Abstract: Post-training papers, model cards, and blog posts often treat scores on a small set of coding benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench and LiveCodeBench) as evidence of broad coding capability, both for research artifacts and user-facing systems. We argue that optimization for these benchmarks leads to measuring task-specific performance, creating a meaning gap between measured scores and claims of general coding ability. We examine this gap with a Django-based case study benchmark suite we create.
  Evaluating foundation models and checkpoints post-trained on SWE-bench trajectories, we find that benchmark rankings frequently fail to generalize. Post-trained checkpoints show little cross-task transfer, and SWE-bench optimization yields limited or no gains on our tasks or on LiveCodeBench. Similarly, fine-tuning on individual Django modalities fails to transfer.
  We conclude that a small number of benchmarks is insufficient for evaluating diverse models under benchmark optimization pressure. We encourage the community to use differentiated evaluation - holistic assessment for frontier models, multi-task suites for research, and human-in-the-loop studies for narrow task applications. Finally, we argue for creating a capability taxonomy and sustained benchmark maintenance, rather than one-off benchmark releases. Without reliable evaluation standards, engineers and researchers using LLMs and agents have to rely on insufficient evidence to make research, development, and deployment decisions.

</details>


### [73] [Robust XGBoosting for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13590)
*Iris Aragón Mladosich,Christophe Croux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了XGBoost的鲁棒性，发现垂直异常值和杠杆点会影响其性能，并提出了基于鲁棒回归中M、S和τ估计器的替代损失函数，结果表明两步过程的MM-XGBoost在鲁棒性和预测精度之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XGBoost虽广泛使用且性能优异，但其标准损失函数（二次损失）对异常值敏感，垂直异常值和杠杆点可能严重影响模型性能，因此需要探索更鲁棒的损失函数。

Method: 在XGBoost中引入基于鲁棒回归估计器（M估计器、S估计器和τ估计器）的替代损失函数，并通过两步过程（MM-XGBoost）在鲁棒性和预测精度之间寻求最佳平衡。

Result: 实验结果表明，MM-XGBoost两步方法提供了鲁棒性与预测精度之间的最佳权衡。

Conclusion: MM-XGBoost是提高XGBoost在存在异常值和杠杆点场景下鲁棒性的有效方案，能在不显著牺牲预测精度的前提下增强模型的抗干扰能力。

Abstract: XGBoost is a very popular and powerful method for prediction. It iteratively fits simple decision trees to the residuals of the previous step. An efficient and scalable implementation is available. The standard loss function for XGBoost is the quadratic loss, but a Huber loss can also be used. In this paper, we study the robustness of XGBoost and show that its performance can be affected by vertical outliers and leverage points. To address this, we explore alternative loss functions, based on M-, S-, and τ -estimators from robust regression. Our results indicate that a two-step procedure, referred to as MM-XGBoost, provides the best trade-off between robustness and prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [74] [Training-Free Knowledge Transfer Across Model Scales through Activation-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13596)
*Jiahe Fan,Si Chen,Yinghao Hou,Aiyuan Zhang,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Activation-Prune-Merge (APM)框架，用于跨尺度异构模型融合，无需逐神经元语义对齐，通过激活引导的剪枝将大模型（donor）的功能组件浓缩并注入小模型（recipient），在16个基准上将3B模型的平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构模型融合旨在结合不同任务、初始化、架构或规模的模型，但跨尺度场景中，小模型从大模型获得能力提升时面临显著的架构不匹配问题，传统方法依赖逐神经元的语义对齐，这是难点且未被充分探索。

Method: APM框架包含三个步骤：1）在donor上构建任务条件化的激活图；2）依据激活图选择关键层、隐藏维度、注意力头和MLP神经元，将donor剪枝至recipient的架构；3）以极小的插值系数将剪枝后的donor切片注入原始recipient，将donor视为功能组件源而非需要精确结构移植的对象。此外还探索了注入比例和多阶段顺序融合。

Result: 在16个涵盖推理、数学、代码生成、指令跟随和分类的基准上，APM将3B recipient的平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%，其中RTE从64.3%提升至82.3%，QNLI从52.3%提升至65.7%，BoolQ从70.8%提升至79.2%。

Conclusion: 当donor的贡献被充分浓缩并精心选择时，跨尺度异构融合无需显式语义对齐也能成功。激活引导的提取提升了可迁移donor切片的质量，同时保持了小比例融合机制的优势。

Abstract: Heterogeneous model fusion seeks to combine models that differ in tasks, initializations, architectures, or scales. We study an underexplored cross-scale setting: improving a small recipient language model with a stronger donor despite substantial architectural mismatch. We ask whether useful capabilities can be transferred without explicit neuron-wise semantic alignment. Building on the observation that truncating a large model to a smaller architecture and injecting it with a tiny mixing weight can already improve the recipient, we propose Activation-Prune-Merge (APM), an activation-guided framework for cross-scale fusion. APM constructs task-conditioned activation maps on the donor, selects salient layers, hidden dimensions, attention heads, and MLP neurons to prune it to the recipient architecture, and injects the resulting donor slice into the original recipient using a micro interpolation coefficient. This formulation treats the donor as a source of concentrated functional components rather than requiring precise structural transplantation. Across 16 benchmarks spanning reasoning, mathematics, code generation, instruction following, and classification, APM improves the overall average accuracy from 55.5% to 60.6% over the original 3B recipient. RTE accuracy increases from 64.3% to 82.3%, QNLI from 52.3% to 65.7%, and BoolQ from 70.8% to 79.2%. Analyses of injection ratios and sequential multi-stage fusion further suggest that activation-guided extraction improves the quality of the transferable donor slice while preserving the small-ratio fusion regime. These results provide evidence that cross-scale heterogeneous fusion can succeed without explicit semantic alignment when the donor contribution is sufficiently concentrated and carefully selected.

</details>


### [75] [Hard Cases, Bad Labels: Testing Error Exposure and Error Location in Uncertainty Sampling Under Bounded Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13601)
*John Myron Uy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过三种噪声条件（干净标签、随机分类噪声、难度依赖噪声）下的对照实验发现，不确定性采样在标签效率上优于随机采样，但其鲁棒性高度依赖于数据集、预算、噪声结构和评估指标，并未发现不确定性采样因获取更多噪声标签而普遍失败的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习通过选择信息量大的样本降低标注成本，但最不确定的样本可能也是最难正确标注的。本文旨在探究不确定性采样失败的原因：是因为获取了更多错误标签，还是因为错误集中在困难区域而尤其有害。

Method: 在三个公开二分类表格数据集上，对比基于边距的不确定性采样与随机采样，在干净标签、随机分类噪声（RCN）和有界难度依赖噪声三种条件下进行实验。使用100对配对种子、9个期望噪声率（0-0.30）、20-120的标注预算，在每个预算下通过交叉验证重新选择逻辑回归的正则化参数。采用暴露匹配的RCN对照以对齐最终累积污染均值，并扩展到预算400的干净标签实验。

Result: 在干净标签下，不确定性采样在所有数据集上使归一化平衡准确率曲线下面积提升1.09至1.77个百分点。难度依赖噪声在六分之八的噪声率下比RCN更大地削弱了这一优势（乳腺癌数据集），但在其他两个数据集上均无此效应。暴露匹配分析未发现结构化错误位置带来普遍额外惩罚的修正证据。在干净的MAGIC数据上，不确定性采样提高了平衡准确率，但在固定假阳性率下降低了平均精度和真正例率。

Conclusion: 不确定性采样在标签效率上具有优势，但其表面上的鲁棒性取决于数据集、预算、噪声结构和评估指标的组合。研究并未证实不确定性采样因获取更多污染标签而系统性失败的假设。

Abstract: Active learning can reduce labeling cost by selecting informative examples, but the most uncertain examples may also be the hardest to label correctly. This study tests whether uncertainty sampling fails because it acquires more corrupted labels or because errors concentrated in difficult regions are especially harmful. Margin-based uncertainty sampling is compared with random sampling under clean labels, random classification noise (RCN), and bounded difficulty-dependent noise on three public binary tabular datasets. The design uses 100 paired seeds, nine expected noise rates from 0 to 0.30, annotation budgets from 20 to 120, and logistic regression with regularization re-selected by cross-validation at every budget. An exposure-matched RCN control aligns mean final acquired corruption, while a clean-label extension reaches budget 400. Under clean labels, uncertainty sampling improved normalized balanced-accuracy area under the learning curve by 1.09 to 1.77 percentage points on all datasets. Difficulty-dependent noise reduced this advantage more than RCN at six of eight rates on Breast Cancer Wisconsin, but at no tested rate on Banknote Authentication or MAGIC Gamma Telescope. Exposure-matched analyses found no corrected evidence for a universal additional penalty from structured error location. On clean MAGIC data, uncertainty sampling improved balanced accuracy while reducing average precision and true-positive rate at fixed false-positive rates. Thus, uncertainty sampling was label-efficient, but its apparent robustness depended on dataset, budget, noise structure, and evaluation metric.

</details>


### [76] [Robust Dual-Model Collaborative Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13628)
*A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KRPRVFL模型，将核风险敏感均值p次幂准则（KRP）引入随机向量功能链接网络（RVFL），替代传统最小二乘目标函数，并引入协作学习机制，以增强模型对噪声标签、异常值和数据不平衡的鲁棒性。实验表明其在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RVFL模型对噪声标签、异常值和不平衡数据敏感，限制了其在真实世界应用中的性能表现，需要一种能够在保持RVFL高效性的同时增强鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 1) 用KRP准则替换标准最小二乘目标函数，自适应降低损坏或不可靠样本的影响；2) 引入协作学习机制，实现模型组件间的自适应交互；3) 利用核诱导特征映射捕获非线性关系，无需显式隐藏层选择。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的大量实验表明，KRPRVFL在准确率、鲁棒性和统计显著性方面一致优于基线模型。

Conclusion: KRPRVFL是一种快速、可扩展且可靠的方法，能有效应对复杂分类任务中的噪声和异常干扰，为挑战性分类问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Random vector functional link (RVFL) networks are lightweight and fast neural models that offer efficient training and strong generalization through randomized hidden-layer weights and direct input-output connections. However, conventional RVFL models are sensitive to noisy labels, outliers, and imbalanced data, which limits their performance in real-world applications. To address these challenges, we propose the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power based RVFL (KRPRVFL) model, which integrates the computational efficiency of RVFL with the robustness of the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power (KRP) criterion. By replacing the standard least-squares objective with a KRP-based loss, KRPRVFL adaptively reduces the influence of corrupted or unreliable samples during training, resulting in improved stability and generalization. Additionally, a collaborative learning mechanism is introduced to enable adaptive interaction among model components, further enhancing robustness in complex and noisy environments. The proposed framework also leverages kernel-induced feature mapping to capture nonlinear relationships without requiring explicit hidden-layer selection, maintaining both efficiency and scalability. Extensive experiments on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets demonstrate that KRPRVFL consistently outperforms baseline models in terms of accuracy, robustness, and statistical significance, highlighting its effectiveness as a fast, scalable, and reliable solution for challenging classification tasks.

</details>


### [77] [Contrastive Learning for Interpretable Anomaly Detection at Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13652)
*Haoyi Jia,Sagar Addepalli,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCA是一个两阶段的事件级异常检测框架，先通过监督对比学习在多样物理过程上学习嵌入空间，再在该空间中用自编码器生成异常分数。在高亮度大型强子对撞机模拟数据上，ORCA相比基线自编码器在灵敏度和可解释性上都有显著提升，嵌入空间的几何结构使异常样本可以通过最大似然模板拟合得到物理上的可解释分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对撞机物理中通用事件级异常检测存在两个反复出现的问题：异常分数难以解释，以及异常分数与能量标度和事例多重数强关联。这限制了异常检测方法在物理搜索中的实际应用价值。

Method: ORCA采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过监督对比学习在多样化的物理过程集合上学习嵌入空间，使已知过程在空间中占据不同区域；第二阶段在此嵌入空间中运行标准自编码器生成事件级异常分数。此外，利用最大似然模板拟合嵌入分布来归属异常事件的物理来源。

Result: 在模拟的HL-LHC条件下，ORCA相比基线自编码器在检测新物理信号的宽度和深度上均有显著提升。模板拟合能准确恢复注入的信号产额（包括训练中未见过的信号），并能将模板库中不存在的信号表征为其最相似的已知过程。

Conclusion: ORCA为对撞机上的可解释异常检测搜索提供了可行路径，其嵌入几何携带更高维的物理信息，相比标准一维输出拟合增强了下游统计分析能力。

Abstract: Generic event-level anomaly detection for collider physics has two recurring problems: anomaly scores are hard to interpret, and they correlate strongly with energy scale and object multiplicity. We present Organized Representation via Contrastive learning for Anomaly detection (ORCA), a two-stage framework that first learns an embedding space via supervised contrastive learning across a diverse set of physics processes, then runs a standard autoencoder in that space to generate event-level anomaly scores. On a simulated dataset consistent with conditions at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, ORCA delivers significant gains in both breadth and depth of sensitivity to new physics signals with respect to a baseline autoencoder architecture. Beyond improved sensitivity, the contrastive embedding makes the anomalous sample interpretable: because known processes occupy distinct regions of the space, a maximum-likelihood template fit to the embedding distributions can attribute events in an anomalous sample to template physics processes with quantified uncertainties. We demonstrate that the fit accurately recovers injected signal yields, including for signals excluded from the training of the embedding, and characterizes signals absent from the template library through the known processes they most resemble. These results establish ORCA as a route to interpretable anomaly detection-based searches at colliders, where the embedding geometry carries higher dimensional physics information compared to standard one-dimensional output fits, enhancing downstream statistical analysis.

</details>


### [78] [The Query Knows What to Forget: A Second Erase Direction for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13668)
*Dhruman Gupta,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出查询派生擦除方向（QED），通过添加与键正交的第二个擦除方向来改进线性注意力机制，解决长上下文中状态干扰导致的检索退化问题，使可用上下文长度约翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力维护固定大小的状态，在长上下文中大量存储项共享该状态，项间干扰会降低检索质量。现有delta规则模型（包括Gated DeltaNet-2）的擦除向量均源于当前token的键，但读取中的干扰是通过查询（query）衡量的，导致擦除步骤无法触及真正需要擦除的部分，存在原理上的不匹配。

Method: 提出查询派生擦除方向（QED）。QED增加一个源于查询（query）且与键（key）正交的第二个擦除方向。在快速权重视角下，键方向的delta编辑无法改变读取中与键正交的部分，QED利用可编辑部分来消除沿查询方向测量的旧状态内容。

Result: QED在超出训练窗口的每个长度上均改善了检索性能，在S-NIAH-1任务上使可用上下文长度大约翻倍。

Conclusion: QED通过引入查询派生的正交擦除方向，解决了delta规则模型中键驱动擦除与查询衡量干扰之间不匹配的问题，有效扩展了线性注意力的有效上下文长度。

Abstract: Linear attention keeps a state of fixed size. At long context, many stored items share this state, and interference between them degrades retrieval. Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2), like every delta-rule model before it, derives its erase vector from the key of the current token. However, the interference in its reads is measured through the query, and the erase step cannot reach it. We introduce the Query-derived Erase Direction (QED). QED adds a second erase direction derived from the query and orthogonal to the key. In the fast-weight view, a key-directed delta edit cannot change the key-orthogonal part of a read. It uses the editable part to cancel old-state content measured along the query. It also improves retrieval at every length past the training window, and it about doubles the usable context length on S-NIAH-1.

</details>


### [79] [From BERT to Frontier Agents: Eight Years of Language-Model Progress, the Collapse of the Capability-Cost Curve, and the Rise of Task-Targeted Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13675)
*Pranav Kumar Kaliaperumal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文总结了2018年10月至2026年7月间AI模型从BERT到大型智能体的演进历程，重点分析了编码能力提升、成本下降和模型专业化分工，并通过数学测试验证了高级采样方法和置信度排序工具的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性地跟踪和评估AI模型在编码任务上的能力提升速度、成本变化趋势以及不同模型的专长领域，同时验证先进推理方法在数学问题上的实际效果。

Method: 对AI模型进行历史纵向对比，评估编码任务解决能力提升幅度；比较不同模型在各类任务下的性能表现与成本；通过Qwen 2.5模型的数学测试对比基础方法与高级采样方法的解题效果，并检验置信度排序工具在50个候选答案中识别正确结果的能力。

Result: 编码问题解决能力自2024年末以来每年提升近6倍；OpenAI的GPT 5.6 Luna以每百万token 1-6美元的价格匹敌旗舰模型；Claude Opus 5领先前端编码，Claude Fable 5擅长仓库级编码，GPT 5.6 Sol主导终端任务；高级采样方法将数学解题率从58%提升至79%；置信度排序工具在前50个选项中正确识别47个正确答案。

Conclusion: AI模型的能力持续快速提升且成本大幅下降，各模型已出现明显的专业化分工趋势；高级推理采样方法和置信度排序工具能有效提升模型在复杂任务中的表现，所有研究材料均已公开。

Abstract: Between October 2018 and July 2026 AI models progressed from simple systems like BERT to massive agents that solve complex math and write software. The ability to resolve real coding issues improved by nearly six times per year since late 2024. During this time costs dropped sharply with OpenAIs budget model GPT 5 point 6 Luna matching flagship capabilities for just one to six dollars per million tokens beating older versions at a fraction of the price. Top performance is now split across specialized models as Claude Opus 5 leads in frontend coding Claude Fable 5 excels at repository level coding and GPT 5 point 6 Sol dominates terminal tasks. In a grade school math test using the Qwen 2 point 5 model basic methods solved 58 of 100 problems while advanced sampling solved up to 79. A confidence ranking tool correctly identified 47 right answers in its top 50 choices proving highly useful for sorting tasks with all research materials made fully public.

</details>


### [80] [EEG-PRISM: Physiologically-Grounded Interpretability of Predictions by EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13676)
*Deeksha M Shama,Punnisa Amornsirikul,Archana Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EEG-PRISM，一种通用的事后可解释性方法，通过线性变换和反向传播规则，将任何EEG基础模型的时-通道归因分数映射到具有生理学意义的频域和源域，无需修改或重新训练底层模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型是EEG分析中AI的下一代进展，但现有可解释AI技术仅提供时-通道空间的归因分数，与临床对EEG的直觉理解不匹配，因此需要一种通用的方法将解释性扩展到其他生理相关领域。

Method: EEG-PRISM利用线性变换和已建立的反向传播规则，将时-通道归因分数映射到替代领域。通过可逆DFT映射到频域，通过近似可逆的EEG生成模型映射到源域。在模拟和真实数据上进行评估，使用五个基础模型和四种AI解释器验证跨域恢复地面真实现象的能力。

Result: 模拟实验中，EEG-PRISM实现了近完美的频谱恢复和69.2%的空间准确性。在癫痫数据中正确判定delta-theta活动最显著，并以50%准确率定位癫痫发作起始区域。在自闭症数据中，将预测性的delta-alpha生物标志物定位到额叶和颞叶区域，与既往研究一致。

Conclusion: EEG-PRISM是一种理论上有依据的事后归因方法，能准确映射到频谱和空间域，支持瞬态事件的窗口级分析（如癫痫发作）和临床相关生物标志物的群体级识别（如自闭症），推动可解释EEG基础模型的发展。

Abstract: Objective: Foundation models represent the next advancement in AI for EEG analysis; however current explainable AI techniques provide attribution scores in the time-channel input space, which is mismatched to clinical intuition about EEG. Thus, there is a critical need for a universal method that can extend the interpretability of any foundation model to alternative and physiologically relevant domains without modifying or retraining the underlying model. Methods: EEG-PRISM leverages linear transformations and established backpropagation rules to map time-channel attribution scores into alternative domains. We derive mappings to the frequency domain via an invertible DFT and to the source domain via an approximately invertible EEG generative model. We evaluate EEG-PRISM in simulated and real data, assessing recovery of ground-truth phenomena across domains with five foundation models and four AI explainers. Results: In simulation, EEG-PRISM achieves near-perfect spectral recovery and 69.2% spatial accuracy. In epilepsy, EEG-PRISM correctly determines that delta-theta activity is most salient and correctly localizes the seizure onset region with 50% accuracy. In autism, EEG-PRISM localizes the predictive delta-alpha biomarkers to frontal and temporal regions, consistent with prior work. Conclusion: EEG-PRISM is a theoretically-grounded post-hoc attribution method with accurate mapping into the spectral and spatial domains. It supports window-level analysis of transient events (e.g., seizures) and group-level identification of clinically relevant biomarkers (e.g., autism), thus advancing interpretable EEG foundation models. Significance: This work enables physiologically-grounded interpretation of EEG foundation models and supports clinically relevant insights such as event localization and biomarker identification.

</details>


### [81] [SAGE: Surrogate-gradient Adaptation via Attention-Guided Entropy for Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13702)
*Kiran Nair,Rodrigue Rizk,KC Santosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAGE机制，利用归一化自注意力熵估计块级不确定性，在训练期间自适应调整Transformer-based SNN的代理梯度斜率，在不改变推理模型的情况下提升训练效果，在CIFAR-10/100上较固定代理梯度基线获得1-2%的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）训练困难，因为不可微的脉冲函数需要代理梯度，而固定形状的代理梯度在不同层和训练阶段可能不最优，影响优化效果。

Method: SAGE从归一化自注意力熵中估计块级不确定性，利用该信号在训练期间动态调整代理梯度斜率；仅在训练时调制代理参数，不改变推理阶段架构和部署成本。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100数据集上，SAGE相比固定代理梯度基线在多个仿真时间步下取得一致的1-2%精度提升。

Conclusion: 注意力衍生不确定性可作为轻量级训练信号，用于Transformer-based SNN的自适应代理梯度学习，为SNN训练优化提供新思路。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer an energy-efficient alternative to conventional deep neural networks by exploiting sparse event-driven computation, but their training remains challenging because the non-differentiable spike function requires surrogate gradients whose fixed shape may be suboptimal across layers and training stages. In this work, we introduce SAGE, an uncertainty-modulated surrogate-gradient mechanism for Transformer-based SNNs. SAGE estimates block-level uncertainty from normalized self-attention entropy and uses this signal to adapt the surrogate-gradient slope during training while leaving the inference model unchanged. By modulating only the training-time surrogate parameter, the proposed method preserves the original architecture and deployment cost while improving optimization flexibility. Experiments on CIFAR-10/100 demonstrate that SAGE achieves improved accuracy over fixed-surrogate baselines, with results up to 1-2\% consistent gains across multiple simulation time steps. These results highlight the potential of attention-derived uncertainty as a lightweight training signal for adaptive surrogate-gradient learning in transformer-based SNNs.

</details>


### [82] [Capacity-Dependent Effects of Data Selection for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13721)
*Cuong Dang,Hoang Anh Just,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了基于似然度的数据选择在推理任务监督微调中的价值，通过1.5B到8B不同规模学生模型的对照实验，发现高似然数据对小模型和早期训练更有利（快速见效），而低似然数据对大模型和更长训练更有利（慢速增益），呈现出依赖于模型容量的"Fast-Fit/Slow-Gain"模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于似然度的响应选择方法认为更接近学生分布的高似然响应能提供更有效的监督信号，但这种直觉是否普遍成立尚不清楚。作者希望验证这一假设的适用边界，探究模型容量和训练时长对似然度数据选择有效性的影响。

Method: 通过受控实验，使用1.5B到8B参数的学生模型，由更强教师模型生成数学推理的监督数据，对比高似然与低似然数据在不同模型规模、不同训练时长下的训练效果；分析学习动态（小模型易陷入浅层或重复行为）；提供容量约束下的蒸馏理论视角。

Result: 发现容量依赖的"Fast-Fit/Slow-Gain"模式：高似然数据带来更快更稳定的早期提升（尤其对小模型），但低似然数据在更大模型和更长训练下逐渐显示优势；小模型难以吸收低似然监督，大模型能更好地向教师分布移动。

Conclusion: 推理任务的有效数据选择应同时考虑模型容量和计算预算，而非遵循单一的"高似然监督更优"的通用偏好。低似然数据并非始终无用，其价值随模型容量增大和训练时间延长而显现。

Abstract: In reasoning supervised fine-tuning, candidate responses for the same instruction can differ substantially in how well they match the student's current distribution. Recent likelihood-based response selection methods suggest that responses closer to the student distribution provide more effective supervision, motivating the hypothesis that high-likelihood responses may generally be preferable for fine-tuning. In this paper, we revisit this intuition and show that the value of likelihood-based data selection depends critically on model capacity and training duration. Through controlled experiments on mathematical reasoning, using students ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters and supervision generated by stronger teacher models, we observe a clear \emph{capacity-dependent} ``{\color{SMALLCOLOR}\textbf{Fast-Fit}} / {\color{LARGECOLOR}\textbf{Slow-Gain}}'' pattern. High-likelihood data provides faster and more stable early improvements, especially for smaller models, but low-likelihood data becomes increasingly beneficial for larger models when training is allowed to continue longer. To explain this phenomenon, we analyze learning dynamics, showing that small models often fail to absorb low-likelihood supervision and instead fall into shallow or repetitive behaviors, while larger models are better able to move toward the teacher distribution under such data. We further provide a capacity-constrained theoretical view of distillation that clarifies how data difficulty, data span, and student capacity jointly govern transfer. Overall, our findings show that effective data selection for reasoning should be aware of model capacity and computing budget rather than based on a single universal preference for high-likelihood supervision.

</details>


### [83] [The Integer Alibi: Localizing Cross-Kernel Divergence in INT8-Quantized LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13756)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过固定除INT8线性核之外的所有条件（检查点、提示词、硬件、推理引擎、解码和量化配置），仅将vLLM中的CUTLASS核替换为Triton核，发现两个核在任何端到端序列上都无法复现彼此的结果。作者通过数学论证（INT32点积在无溢出下精确且与顺序无关）排除了累加器差异，并通过逐层注入相同操作数定位到缩放应用和输出舍入环节，最终以探针检查点恢复端到端逐位一致。FP8 GEMM表现出不同特征：差异随归约深度增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区普遍假设实现相同INT8 GEMM接口的不同GPU内核是可互换的，但这一假设从未被严格检验。作者想要验证这一假设，并弄清内核替换后结果分歧的根源。

Method: 在vLLM中保持检查点、提示词、硬件、推理引擎、解码和量化配置完全固定，仅交换INT8线性核（CUTLASS vs Triton）。通过数学证明排除INT32累加器差异，将两个核的逐层输出对齐比较，定位到缩放应用与舍入环节；以探针检查点验证修复效果；同时对比FP8 GEMM作为对照；用教师强制的回放分析翻转与logit边际的关系。

Result: 在1.7B和8B模型上（196/252层）逐层注入相同操作数，power-of-two缩放下实现逐位一致（196/196和252/252预注册通过），真实缩放下观察到至多一个bfloat16间隔的差异。端到端比较中0/8、0/16和0/64的序列完全不一致；探针检查点恢复后达到8/8和16/16逐位一致。INT8差异率保持在百万分之几且在64倍K范围内均在一个间距内，而FP8差异随归约深度增长。logit边际预测翻转风险的ROC-AUC达0.94。

Conclusion: 相同接口的GPU内核并非天然可互换，INT8差异源于内核在精确累加后的缩放应用与舍入策略，而非累加器本身。作者将发布预注册方案、逐层预测、内核选择证据清单及一致性测试流程，为内核可互换性提供可操作的验证手段。

Abstract: Two GPU kernels implementing the same scaled INT8 GEMM interface are usually treated as interchangeable. We test that assumption: holding the checkpoint, prompts, hardware, inference engine, decoding, and quantization configuration fixed, we swap only the INT8 linear kernel (CUTLASS versus Triton) inside vLLM. At 1.7B each arm reproduces itself bit-for-bit across cold restarts, yet the arms agree on no sequence in any end-to-end comparison we ran (0/8, 0/16, and 0/64). What makes this more than a benchmark discrepancy is an integer alibi: for shared INT8 operands under a verified no-overflow bound, the INT32 dot product is exact and order-independent, so the accumulator cannot be the source of any difference. Feeding both kernels identical operands from every linear layer of Qwen3-1.7B and 8B (196 and 252 layers), we find bit-identical outputs under power-of-two scales, confirming a pinned prediction list 196/196 and 252/252 (pre-registered at 1.7B, pinned but not blind at 8B), and observed differences of at most one bfloat16 spacing under the checkpoints' real scales. This localizes the divergence to scale application and output rounding after the exact accumulator. Applied as a probe checkpoint, the same intervention restores end-to-end bitwise agreement (8/8 and 16/16 sequences). Cross-implementation FP8 GEMM shows a different signature: both the prevalence and the magnitude of differences grow with reduction depth, while the INT8 fraction stays at parts per million and within one spacing over a 64x range of K. Teacher-forced replay ties layers to tokens: flips concentrate at small logit margins, which predict flip risk with ROC-AUC 0.94 on 16,384 positions. We will release the pre-registration, per-layer predictions, manifests with kernel-selection evidence, and a conformance procedure that turns these controls into a concrete check for kernel interchangeability.

</details>


### [84] [CutClean: Neural Network Pruning for Privacy-Preserving Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13773)
*Leonardo Magliolo,Vito Paolo Pastore,Giuseppe Valenzise,Enzo Tartaglione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CutClean，一种隐私感知的剪枝方法，通过在神经网络每个模块放置辅助线性隐私头来量化隐私信息泄漏，并施加递增稀疏度来移除敏感属性泄漏，在提高模型稀疏性的同时保护隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在高风险场景中部署日益增多，隐私泄露问题日益严重。现有研究表明隐私泄露可能在没有传统数据集偏差（表示不平衡）的情况下发生，这构成重大隐私风险，需要新的隐私保护方法。

Method: CutClean在每个网络模块处放置辅助线性隐私头来量化信息泄漏，然后对网络施加递增级别的稀疏化（剪枝），以移除私有属性泄漏，泄漏程度通过连接在最后一个模块上的隐私头准确率来衡量。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法能有效最小化私有信息流，同时实现高稀疏率和保持分类目标准确率。

Conclusion: CutClean能够在不牺牲分类任务性能的前提下，通过剪枝同时实现模型稀疏化和隐私保护，为隐私感知的深度学习模型部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications with growing privacy leakage concerns. We show that this privacy leakage can occur even in the absence of representation imbalances that lead to traditional dataset biases. This poses significant privacy risks when deploying models that process sensitive attributes. In this context, we propose CutClean, a privacy-aware pruning method that allows to reduce privacy information flow through the network, while increasing its sparsity. Our approach employs auxiliary linear privacy heads placed at each network's block to quantify information leakage, and further applies increasing levels of sparsity to remove the private attribute leakage, measured in terms of the accuracy of the privacy head attached to the last block. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach effectively minimizes private information flow while achieving high sparsity rates and preserving classification target accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [PPAPlace: Differentiable Cross-Stage Objectives for Chip Placement Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13790)
*Ruogu Chen,Jie Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PPAPlace提出了一种基于时序驱动的可微代理模型，从宏单元和标准单元布局预测布线后PPA指标，并以两种方式利用预测梯度来改善芯片宏布局的时序表现。在两个基准测试电路上，该方法相比分层基线将WNS和TNS分别平均提升22%和51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宏布局方法普遍以半周长线长（HPWL）作为主要优化目标，但研究表明HPWL与布线后时序指标（如WNS/TNS）几乎零相关，导致所有评估的AI布局器相比分层基线都出现PPA退化。已有跨阶段预测器的尝试仅关注宏观表征且使用布线前指标作为训练标签，无法准确反映最终时序排名，因此需要一个能够预测布线后PPA的代理模型。

Method: PPAPlace包含一个双流预测器，结合芯片网表上的图注意力网络与布局网格上的空间卷积，以全局布线后标签训练预测WNS和TNS。预测梯度端到端回传到单元坐标，并以两种方式利用：作为共目标注入分析布局器的优化循环（PPAPlace-CoOpt），以及作为后布局精化步骤，通过投影梯度下降调整宏单元位置（PPAPlace-Refine）。作者还对十个电路在四个设计流程阶段进行了标签保真度研究。

Result: 在五个排除于训练集的ChiPBench测试电路上，PPAPlace在不重新训练测试电路的前提下，相比分层基线将平均WNS和TNS分别提升22%和51%，同时保持了功耗和可布线性。

Conclusion: 以全局布线后指标作为标签训练时序驱动的可微代理模型，能够有效弥合布局与布线后时序之间的差距，为宏布局提供时序敏感的优化信号，显著改善芯片PPA。

Abstract: Macro placement significantly affects a chip's post-route performance, power, and area (PPA). Most placement methods optimize half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) as the primary objective. However, recent benchmarking shows a near-zero correlation between HPWL and post-route timing metrics such as the worst negative slack (WNS) and total negative slack (TNS). As a result, all six evaluated artificial intelligence (AI) placers degraded PPA relative to the hierarchical baseline. Recent efforts have tried to train cross-stage predictors to close this gap. However, existing methods focus on macro-only representations and use pre-route metrics as training labels. A label fidelity study of ten circuits at four design flow stages reveals that HPWL and pre-route timing poorly reflect final post-route timing rankings. In contrast, post-global-routing achieves the best balance between final timing fidelity and label generation cost-effectiveness. Based on this finding, PPAPlace is a timing-driven differentiable surrogate predicting post-route PPA from macro and standard-cell placements. The surrogate is a dual-stream predictor that combines graph attention over the chip netlist with spatial convolution over the placement grid. It is trained on post-global-routing labels. The predicted WNS and TNS gradients flow end-to-end back to cell coordinates. PPAPlace exploits these gradients in two ways: as a co-objective injected into an analytical placer's optimization loop (PPAPlace-CoOpt), and as a post-placement refinement step that adjusts macro positions via projected gradient descent (PPAPlace-Refine). On five ChiPBench test circuits excluded from training, PPAPlace improves average WNS and TNS by 22\% and 51\% over the hierarchical baseline while preserving power and routability, using the same predictor without test-circuit retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/ValleyC/PPAPlace.

</details>


### [86] [Recent Advances in Deep Learning-Based Drug-Target Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13797)
*Jafin Khan,Md Hossain Shuvo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对药物-靶点结合亲和力预测的近期机器学习方法进行了全面综述和比较分析，回顾了代表性深度学习方法、七个常用基准数据集和评估指标，指出当前方法在数据集偏差、冷启动场景泛化能力等方面存在局限，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管药物-靶点结合亲和力预测在药物发现中至关重要，且近期深度学习技术有诸多进展，但准确预测仍是一个开放研究领域。作者希望通过系统性综述和比较分析，识别现有方法的优势、局限和研究空白，推动该领域进一步发展的其核心动机。

Method: 作者系统回顾了使用通用基准数据集和评估指标的近期深度学习方法，涵盖多种神经网络架构（如图神经网络、Transformer等）和表示策略；同时分析了七个广泛使用的基准数据集和常用评估指标，从数据集偏差、评估设置、冷启动场景等维度对方法进行比较分析。

Result: 分析表明，尽管许多方法在标准基准上报告了强劲性能，但其有效性常受限于数据集偏差和有限的评估设置；大多数方法在冷启动场景下性能显著下降，反映出泛化能力的不足；同时识别出数据集不平衡、缺乏标准化评估、真实世界适用性有限等关键问题。

Conclusion: 当前方法存在数据集不平衡、评估标准不统一、真实应用受限和冷启动泛化不足等局限。未来研究应关注更好的数据集设计、更稳健的评估方法、冷启动问题改进以及多模态表示的整合。

Abstract: Computational approaches to drug discovery involve multiple sub-problems, and among them, drug-target binding affinity prediction plays an important role. Despite recent advances, accurately predicting binding affinity remains an open research area. The major objective of our paper is to perform a comprehensive review and comparative analysis of recent machine learning methods for drug-target binding affinity prediction, with a focus on identifying strengths, limitations, and research gaps. We review representative recent deep learning approaches that use common benchmark datasets and evaluation metrics, covering a range of neural network architectures and representation strategies. In addition, we analyze seven widely used benchmark datasets and commonly adopted evaluation metrics for drug-target binding affinity prediction. Our analysis indicates that although many methods report strong performance on standard benchmarks, their effectiveness is often influenced by dataset bias and limited evaluation settings. Furthermore, most methods exhibit reduced performance in cold-start scenarios, highlighting challenges in generalization. We identify several limitations of current approaches, including dataset imbalance, the lack of standardized evaluation, limited real-world applicability, and challenges in cold-start scenarios. We also discuss future research directions, including better dataset design, more robust evaluation methods, improved handling of cold-start problems, and the integration of multimodal representations.

</details>


### [87] [Dynamic Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing with Online Requests: Event-Driven Transformer--DRL and Rolling-Horizon Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13799)
*Faezeh Ardali,Gerald M. Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向动态多车场车辆路径问题的事件驱动学习与基准测试框架，对比了Masked MLP和Transformer两种学习策略与动态插入启发式及滚动时域优化方法，结果发现最近的可行启发式在综合表现上最优，学习策略虽推理快且可扩展但未超越最强启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态多车场车辆路径问题中存在逐步揭示的需求和不断变化的车辆状态，传统方法难以同时兼顾路由效率、服务响应性、稳定性和在线计算成本，需要系统性的学习与基准框架来评估不同方法的优劣。

Method: 采用事件驱动框架，训练Masked MLP和Transformer策略，分别通过行为克隆和近端策略优化（PPO）进行学习；利用确定性可行性掩码防止无效的车-需求分配；通过固定前缀/灵活后缀的路线承诺机制保护已完成、进行中和近期决策，并分别衡量车辆重新分配和重新排序；与动态插入启发式和限时滚动时域优化进行对比。

Result: 在20场景策略基准测试中，所有方法均无无效动作完成全部请求，但最近的可行启发式获得了最低的平均目标值，在路由质量、等待时间、稳定性、完工时间和运行时间上均优于学习策略。PPO对MLP影响甚微，对Transformer有平均提升但种子方差更大。滚动时域方法等待时间和完工时间最低但计算成本显著更高。学习策略保持毫秒级决策并可迁移至80个请求的实例，但未超越最强启发式。

Conclusion: 没有任何单一方法能在路由效率、服务响应性、稳定性和在线计算方面全面最优；学习策略的优势在于快速决策和可扩展性，但启发式方法在当前场景下表现更佳，未来需要进一步改进学习策略以缩小与启发式的差距。

Abstract: This paper presents an event-driven learning and benchmarking framework for the Dynamic Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing Problem with progressively revealed requests and evolving vehicle states. Masked MLP and Transformer policies are trained through behavior cloning and proximal policy optimization. Deterministic feasibility masking prevents invalid vehicle--request assignments, while fixed-prefix/flexible-suffix route commitments protect completed, active, and near-term decisions and separately measure vehicle reassignment and resequencing. The learned policies are compared with dynamic insertion heuristics and time-limited rolling-horizon optimization. In a 20-scenario policy benchmark, all methods completed every request without invalid actions, but nearest feasible achieved the lowest mean objective and outperformed the learned policies in routing quality, waiting time, stability, makespan, and runtime. Across five independent training runs, PPO had little average effect on the MLP and improved the Transformer on average, although with greater seed variability. Under the common protocol, nearest feasible achieved the lowest combined objective and route disruption, whereas rolling horizon achieved the lowest waiting times and makespan at substantially higher computational cost. The learned policies retained millisecond-level decisions and transferred to instances with up to 80 requests without retraining, but did not outperform the strongest heuristic. No single method was best across routing efficiency, service responsiveness, stability, and online computation.

</details>


### [88] [Stochastic Control Policies for Robust Molecular Transition Path Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13800)
*Jingqian Liu,Yu-Hsiang Wang,Yanru Qu,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种随机策略（FS-TPS和LaS-TPS）来改进基于rollout的过渡路径采样（TPS）方法，通过在控制策略中引入随机性来提升探索能力和优化鲁棒性，并在三个生物分子系统上验证了其相对于确定性策略基线的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于rollout的控制方法虽然能产生更物理合理的路径，但存在性能不稳定和强依赖随机种子的问题。作者希望通过重新审视随机性放置这一设计选择，来改善路径空间探索效率和优化鲁棒性。

Method: 将rollout-based控制重新表述为学习路径空间的提议分布，开发两种随机策略：FS-TPS直接对控制策略输出参数化状态依赖的高斯分布；LaS-TPS采样紧凑的潜在控制变量并解码为结构化、跨原子相关的力变化。在丙氨酸二肽、chignolin和BBL三个生物分子系统上进行多种子实验评估。

Result: 随机策略在过渡成功率和路径质量上持续优于确定性策略基线，同时显著降低了对随机初始化的敏感性。

Conclusion: 随机性放置是rollout-based TPS方法中重要的设计选择，通过引入适当的随机策略可以有效改善探索能力、提升过渡采样成功率和路径质量，并增强方法在不同随机初始化下的稳定性。

Abstract: Transition path sampling (TPS) aims to efficiently generate rare molecular transition trajectories between metastable states and is essential for understanding biomolecular mechanisms. Beyond traditional molecular dynamics (MD)-based sampling, machine learning has become central to state-of-the-art TPS. One major class of methods learns control forces during explicit MD rollouts. By preserving the underlying molecular dynamics, these methods tend to produce more physically plausible trajectories than endpoint-conditioned generators that construct paths directly. However, rollout-based control methods have been reported to exhibit unstable and strongly seed-dependent performance. We recast rollout-based control as learning a path-space proposal distribution and investigate stochasticity placement as a design choice for improving exploration and optimization robustness. We develop two stochastic policies: FS-TPS, which directly parameterizes a state-dependent Gaussian distribution over the control policy output, and LaS-TPS, which samples a compact latent control variable and decodes it into structured, cross-atom-correlated force variation. We conduct extensive multi-seed experiments on three biomolecular systems of increasing size: alanine dipeptide, chignolin, and BBL, a fast-folding protein. Stochastic policies consistently improve transition success and path quality over deterministic-policy baselines while substantially reducing sensitivity to random initialization.

</details>


### [89] [HI-MeshGraphNets: Efficient and Accurate Mesh-based Physics Learning with Hierarchical Multi-scale Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13827)
*SiHun Lee,Dong-Hyuk Park,Taesoo Bang,Seung-Hoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HI-MGN，一种多尺度扩展的MeshGraphNets，通过最远点采样和Voronoi划分构建层次化粗化图，利用粗图上的消息传递实现长距离信息高效传播，并通过学习式插值网络重建细粒度特征，在结构力学和流体力学基准上取得更高精度且降低训练时间和内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的平面图神经网络在大规模高保真网格上存在信息传播效率低的问题：消息传递每层仅传播一跳，处理长距离交互需要深层网络，导致计算开销大、内存占用高，并且容易出现过平滑（over-smoothing）现象。

Method: HI-MGN将MeshGraphNets的平面处理器替换为层次化多尺度处理器：利用最远点采样（farthest-point sampling）和Voronoi划分对图进行粗化，同时保留原始网格拓扑；在粗图上进行消息传递使信息在更少的层数内跨越更大几何距离；最后通过可学习的图插值网络将粗尺度特征重建为细分辨率特征。

Result: 在三个结构力学和流体力学基准测试中，HI-MGN相比MeshGraphNets和Bi-Stride Multi-Scale GNN实现了更高的精度，同时减少了训练时间和峰值内存使用。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的层次化消息传递与可学习的粗到细插值相结合，为可扩展的基于网格的物理代理建模提供了有效且实用的框架。

Abstract: Machine-learned physical surrogate models have become promising alternatives to mesh-based numerical solvers. Among them, graph neural networks (GNNs) are well suited for representing simulation meshes and learning nodal state evolution through message passing. However, conventional flat message passing becomes inefficient on large, high-fidelity meshes because information propagates only one hop per layer, requiring deep processors for long-range interactions and increasing computational cost, memory usage, and the risk of over-smoothing.
  To address this limitation, we propose Hierarchical Interpolating MeshGraphNets (HI-MGN), a multiscale extension of MeshGraphNets for efficient long-range communication on unstructured meshes. HI-MGN replaces the flat processor with a hierarchical multiscale processor that coarsens graphs using farthest-point sampling and Voronoi partitioning while preserving the original mesh topology. Message passing on coarse graphs enables information to travel over larger geometric distances with fewer layers, and a learned graph interpolation network reconstructs fine-resolution features.
  Across three structural and fluid benchmarks, HI-MGN achieves improved accuracy compared with MeshGraphNets and the Bi-Stride Multi-Scale GNN while reducing training time and peak memory usage. The results show that topology-aware hierarchical message passing and learned coarse-to-fine interpolation provide an effective and practical framework for scalable mesh-based physics surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [90] [Federated Prompt Learning: A Unified Framework, Empirical Analysis, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13844)
*Qinglin Yang,Chen Qiu,Hongyuan Zhang,Pengdeng Li,Yuan Liu,Zhihong Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联邦提示学习（FPL）进行了全面综述，系统地回顾了将联邦学习范式与大型语言模型相结合的最新进展，围绕三大研究问题展开：FPL的基本动机、特征与使能技术；FPL在性能、通信效率、计算开销、可扩展性、个性化和异构处理方面的权衡；以及剩余的安全、隐私、鲁棒性和系统挑战及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在训练和部署中面临高昂计算成本、数据集中化和隐私担忧等挑战，而联邦学习提供了一种去中心化的训练范式。本文旨在通过综述联邦提示学习（FPL）来回答该领域的关键研究问题，帮助读者理解FPL如何推动相关研究。

Method: 本文采用系统综述方法，从三个研究问题（RQ1基础概念、RQ2性能权衡、RQ3安全挑战）出发，全面审视现有的FPL方法，覆盖模型全生命周期（预训练、微调和实际应用），并讨论安全问题、隐私问题、鲁棒性问题及现有防御机制。

Result: 系统性地整理了FPL领域的方法分类，展示了FPL与传统联邦学习及全模型联邦微调的区别，分析了各类方法在多个维度上的权衡关系，总结了现有的防御机制以及尚未解决的安全、隐私、鲁棒性和系统层面挑战。

Conclusion: FPL是隐私保护型大语言模型训练与推理的前景方向，但仍存在诸多开放挑战，未来研究需要进一步解决安全性、隐私性、鲁棒性和系统实现等方面的问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become core components of cloud-based intelligent services in academia and industry, yet their training and deployment are hindered by high computational costs, data centralization, and privacy concerns. Federated learning (FL) offers a decentralized training paradigm that enables clients to collaboratively train a learning model without sharing raw data, making it a promising solution for privacy-preserving LLM training and reasoning. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of federated prompt learning (FPL) to review recent advances in integrating the federated learning paradigm and large language models, answering the following research questions: RQ1: The fundamental motivations, characteristics, and enabling technologies of FPL, and how it differs from conventional FL and full-model federated fine-tuning; RQ2: The trade-offs FPL approaches exhibit in performance, communication efficiency, computational overhead, scalability, personalization, and heterogeneity handling; RQ3: The remaining security, privacy, robustness, and system challenges, along with key future research directions. To this end, we systematically examine existing FPL methods across the full model lifecycle: pre-training, fine-tuning, and practical applications, while discussing security, privacy, and robustness issues and summarizing existing defense mechanisms. Finally, we highlight open challenges and future directions, aiming to help readers understand how the insights drive research in FPL.

</details>


### [91] [Geometric Filtering of LLM-Generated Samples for Few-Shot Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13866)
*Benjamín Schindler,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于几何过滤的框架，通过计算LLM生成样本与真实类别样本在句嵌入空间中的欧氏距离，筛选几何一致的合成数据用于文本分类训练。在13个数据集、5种分类器、10种增强方法、6700+配置下，相比SMOTE提升+2.61个百分点（p<0.0001，Cohen's d=0.95，88.9%胜率），并泛化到命名实体识别任务（+9.26pp，100%胜率）。研究发现最简单的距离过滤方法优于复杂的多准则替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的合成训练数据质量参差不齐：部分样本落在正确的类别嵌入区域，而其他样本则位于边缘或跨类别区域。因此需要一种方法来筛选高质量合成样本，以提升文本分类性能。

Method: 提出几何过滤框架：1）在句嵌入空间中计算每个LLM生成样本到真实类别样本的欧氏距离；2）仅选择几何一致的候选样本；3）设计软加权机制，将过滤得分转换为样本权重用于分类器训练。该框架无需修改过滤模块即可泛化到命名实体识别任务。

Result: 在13个数据集、5种分类器、10种增强方法、超过6700种配置下，方法相比SMOTE提升+2.61个百分点（p<0.0001，Cohen's d=0.95，88.9%胜率）。在命名实体识别任务上实现+9.26个百分点的提升（100%胜率）。在来自4个提供商的5种LLM上表现稳健。

Conclusion: 基于嵌入空间距离的简单过滤方法在合成数据筛选中优于复杂方案，为LLM合成训练数据的质量筛选提供了有效且泛化的几何方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate synthetic training data for text classification, but the quality of generated samples is heterogeneous: some fall in correct class regions of the embedding space while others land in peripheral or cross-class zones. We propose a geometric filtering framework that evaluates each LLM-generated sample by its Euclidean distance to real class examples in a sentence embedding space, selecting only geometrically consistent candidates. A soft weighting mechanism transforms filter scores into sample weights for classifier training. Evaluated across 13 datasets, 5 classifiers, 10 augmentation methods, and over 6,700 configurations, our method achieves +2.61 percentage points (pp) over SMOTE ($p<0.0001$, Cohen's $d=0.95$, 88.9% win rate). The approach generalizes to named entity recognition (+9.26pp, 100% win rate) without filter modification, and is robust across 5 LLMs from 4 providers. A key finding is that the simplest distance-based filter consistently outperforms complex multi-criteria alternatives.

</details>


### [92] [Variation Brownian Kernel Ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13882)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出变分布朗核阶梯（VBKL），一个路径原子函数空间框架，将非线性递归字典构造与线性变分叠加分离。通过布朗再生核希尔伯特空间中的单位球轮廓递归组合构造每个原子，并以符号测度变分包络构建完整空间。研究者建立了变分控制的Hölder正则性、紧性与可达性，证明了深度在局部非退化条件下的严格增长，并推导了Rademacher与泛化界以及两阶段逼近器的误差界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络的优势并非绝对，而是依赖于表示复杂度的分配方式。现有理论框架难以清晰分离递归字典构造与线性叠加的作用，缺乏对深度增益的严谨刻画。

Method: 引入变分布朗核阶梯（VBKL）框架：每个原子通过布朗再生核希尔伯特空间递归组合单位球轮廓；完整空间为完成字典的符号测度变分包络。将递归字典识别为布朗回拉RKHS球的并集，结合布朗二次混沌、符号阈值迹和VC熵推导泛化界，并通过离散化外测度与外部布朗轮廓构造两阶段逼近器。

Result: 建立了变分控制的Hölder正则性、紧性与可达性；在局部非退化条件下证明深度严格增长；得到M^{-1/2}+m^{-1/2}误差界、尖锐插值常数√(A/2)以及每次评估至多2M个活跃外部轮廓基贡献。实验验证了逼近机制并显示出有利的有限数据精度-复杂度权衡。

Conclusion: VBKL框架为深度表示的优势提供了严格的理论基础，表明深度增益取决于表示复杂度的设定方式，并通过变分路径原子的视角统一了递归构造与线性叠加的分析。

Abstract: Claims about the benefit of depth depend on the complexity assigned to a representation. We introduce the \emph{Variation Brownian Kernel Ladder} (VBKL), a path-atomic function-space framework that separates nonlinear recursive dictionary construction from linear variation superposition. Starting from linear projections, each atom recursively composes unit-ball profiles from the Brownian reproducing kernel Hilbert space; the full VBKL space is then the signed-measure variation hull of the completed dictionary. We identify each recursive dictionary as a union of Brownian pullback RKHS balls and establish variation-controlled Hölder regularity, compactness and attainment, and strict growth with depth under a local non-degeneracy condition whose trace lies in the support of the input measure. For associated finite lower-support architectures, we derive Rademacher and generalization bounds through Brownian quadratic chaos, signed threshold traces, and VC entropy. We also construct two-stage approximants by discretizing the outer measure and the selected outer Brownian profiles, obtaining an $M^{-1/2}+m^{-1/2}$ error bound, a sharp interpolation constant $\sqrt{A/2}$, and at most $2M$ active outer-profile basis contributions per evaluation. Controlled experiments illustrate the approximation mechanisms and indicate a favorable limited-data accuracy--complexity trade-off.

</details>


### [93] [Fashion Outfit Generation via Unified Sequential Composition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13888)
*Kaicheng Pang,Xingxing Zou,Ruohan Xu,Waikeung Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一顺序组合模型（USCM）与潜在扩展蒙特卡洛树搜索（LE-MCTS）机制，将时尚套装生成任务形式化为有限时域确定性马尔可夫决策过程，在Polyvore Outfits等数据集上实现了最先进的生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚套装生成面临两大挑战：美学兼容性的非单调与隐式特性，以及庞大的组合搜索空间。现有的方法难以同时处理集合级别的兼容性和高效的物品检索，因此需要一种能够联合建模并指导组合搜索的新框架。

Method: 将任务形式化为约束集成生成（CEG），建模为有限时域确定性马尔可夫决策过程；提出统一顺序组合模型（USCM）联合建模集合级兼容性与潜在组合意图；基于USCM学习到的先验，设计潜在扩展蒙特卡洛树搜索（LE-MCTS）机制来处理组合过程中的物品检索，平衡局部美学协同与全局结构平衡。

Result: 在PolyVore Outfits数据集上进行广泛实验，并在iFashion和PolyvoreU数据集上进行零样本评估，在独立人类偏好评估、自动化美学代理指标和结构有效性度量上均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 提出的USCM结合LE-MCTS框架有效解决了受约束时尚套装生成问题，通过联合建模兼容性与潜在意图，并在搜索中平衡局部与全局考虑，实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: The task of synthesizing stylistically coherent fashion outfits from massive item libraries, known as fashion outfit generation, remains a non-trivial challenge, primarily due to the non-monotonic and implicit nature of aesthetic compatibility, coupled with the exponentially large combinatorial search space. In this paper, we formalize this task as Constrained Ensemble Generation (CEG) and model it as a finite-horizon deterministic Markov Decision Process. To address CEG in fashion, we propose the Unified Sequential Composition Model (USCM), which jointly models set-level compatibility and latent composition intents. Guided by USCM's learned priors, a Latent Expansion Monte Carlo Tree Search (LE-MCTS) mechanism is proposed to handle item retrieval during composition, balancing local aesthetic synergy with global structural balance. Extensive experiments on the Polyvore Outfits dataset, along with zero-shot evaluations on the iFashion and PolyvoreU datasets, demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance across independent human preference evaluations, automated aesthetic proxies, and structural validity metrics for constrained fashion outfit generation.

</details>


### [94] [MedMix: Specialization-Consistent Federated Sparse MoEs under Modality Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13911)
*Adiba Orzikulova,Dong Min Kim,Jaehong Yoon,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MedMix，一个面向联邦多模态医学AI的语义对齐框架，通过模态上下文感知路由、共识引导路由对齐和客户端自适应专家聚合，解决跨客户端模态异质性下稀疏混合专家模型的路由分歧与专家专业化错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦多模态医学AI面临客户端级和样本级两种模态异质性，稀疏MoE虽可用于模态自适应计算，但在跨客户端模态异质性下，本地路由策略会发散导致专家专业化不兼容，标准聚合无法对齐或覆盖专家专业化。

Method: MedMix包含三个核心组件：1) 客户端侧模态上下文感知路由，利用token的模态身份、位置和不完整度上下文指导专家选择；2) 跨客户端共识引导路由对齐，在服务器端为共享模态模式构建共识锚点并对齐各客户端路由分布；3) 客户端自适应专家聚合，利用客户端特异的模态模式原型匹配和聚合功能相似的专家。

Result: 在真实多模态医学数据集上的实验表明，MedMix在多种模态异质性和模态不完整性设置下取得最佳平均F1分数，在严重异质性场景下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: MedMix通过协调跨客户端的路由和专家专业化，有效解决了联邦多模态稀疏MoE在模态异质性下的脆弱性问题，验证了语义对齐框架在联邦多模态医学AI中的有效性。

Abstract: Federated multimodal medical AI faces modality heterogeneity at both the client and sample levels: clients may systematically lack access to specific modality types, while individual records within the same client may contain different partial modality subsets. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are a promising remedy for modality-adaptive computation, but their use in federated learning is fragile under cross-client modality heterogeneity, where locally learned routing policies can diverge across clients and drive experts toward incompatible specializations. Different clients may assign the same observed modality configuration to different experts, or train similarly indexed experts on different missing-modality configurations, causing standard aggregation to misalign or overwrite the expert specialization that sparse MoEs are intended to learn. To address this challenge, we propose MedMix, a semantic-alignment framework for federated multimodal sparse MoEs that coordinates cross-client routing and expert specialization using modality context. At the client side, MedMix uses modality-context-aware routing to guide expert selection using each token's modality identity, position, and incompleteness context. Across clients, it uses consensus-guided routing alignment to construct server-side consensus anchors for shared modality patterns and align local routing distributions across clients. Complementing these routing mechanisms, client-adaptive expert aggregation leverages client-specific modality-pattern prototypes to match and aggregate functionally similar experts across clients. Experiments on real-world multimodal medical datasets show that MedMix achieves the best average F1 across diverse modality heterogeneity and modality incompleteness settings, with especially clear gains under severe heterogeneity.

</details>


### [95] [Hybrid Quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Privacy-Aware Federated Biosignal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13914)
*Chun-Hua Lin,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Yu-Chao Hsu,Kuo-Chung Peng,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chi-Sheng Chen,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,En-Jui Kuo,Hsi-Sheng Goan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评测了一种混合量子启发式Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（HQKAN）与多层感知机（MLP）在联邦学习框架下进行心律失常分类的性能。在MIT-BIH和INCART数据集上，HQKAN在多数聚合指标和少数类别指标上表现更优，同时显著减少了可训练参数数量和通信开销，为隐私保护的生物信号联邦学习提供了更紧凑高效的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电信号属于敏感生物医学数据，限制集中式训练，联邦学习虽能保护隐私，但面临客户端样本少、标签不均衡和non-IID数据分布等挑战，需要兼具通信效率和跨客户端分布鲁棒性的分类器。

Method: 在联邦平均（FedAvg）框架下，将混合量子启发式Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（HQKAN）与多层感知机（MLP）进行对比，在MIT-BIH数据集上进行五类心律失常分类、在INCART数据集上进行三类分类，并在多种客户端配置下评估。

Result: HQKAN在多数聚合指标和少数类别指标上优于MLP；在MIT-BIH上减少37.35%可训练参数、降低24.89%通信成本；在INCART上分别减少44.81%和36.41%。

Conclusion: HQKAN以更紧凑的模型规模和更低的通信开销，为隐私感知的联邦生物信号学习提供了比MLP基线更稳健且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) recordings are sensitive biomedical data, limiting the ability of hospitals and wearable devices to share raw signals for centralized model training. Federated learning addresses this practical privacy constraint by enabling collaborative model training while keeping raw biosignal data at their respective sources. However, federated ECG classification remains challenging due to limited client-side samples, imbalanced arrhythmia labels, and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data across clients. These constraints require classifiers that are both communication-efficient and robust to cross-client distribution shifts. In this work, we evaluate a hybrid quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold network (HQKAN) against a multilayer perceptron (MLP) for five-class arrhythmia classification on the MIT-BIH dataset and three-class classification on the INCART dataset under federated averaging (FedAvg). Across multiple client configurations, HQKAN improves most aggregate and minority-class metrics while using 37.35% fewer trainable parameters and reducing communication cost by 24.89% on MIT-BIH; on INCART, it achieves corresponding reductions of 44.81% and 36.41%. These results indicate that HQKAN offers a compact, communication-efficient and robust alternative to the MLP baseline for privacy-aware federated learning on biosignal data.

</details>


### [96] [High-dimensional nonparametric changepoint detection via low-rank degree-two density projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13922)
*Guoqing Zhang,Zhaixin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于表示（representation-based）的高维分布变化检测方法，通过构造对称特征矩阵将密度估计转化为矩阵均值估计，利用低秩投影跳变而非稀疏性来检测变化点，并提供了完整的非渐近理论分析与计算可行的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维分布变化检测的难点在于前后密度均无法参数化指定。现有方法通常依赖稀疏性假设或均值变化检测，无法捕捉纯依赖结构（dependence）的变化。作者希望提出一种不依赖参数化密度假设、能检测低维结构变化（如相关性变化）的通用方法。

Method: 构造对称特征矩阵H_2(X)，使M(f)=E_f H_2(X)成为密度二阶正交投影的等距编码；对矩阵CUSUM进行秩r截断扫描，利用投影跳变的低秩性；提出种子窄过阈值（narrowest-over-threshold）多变化点程序；交叉拟合标量细化方法在一折学习变化方向、另一折定位，实现误差O(κ^{-2})；利用依赖矩阵Bernstein不等式推广到β-混合序列。

Result: 主导随机项规模为√(rd log(nd))；多变化点精确恢复通过归纳证明；交叉拟合细化达到~O_P(κ^{-2})误差且匹配Le Cam下界（最优至对数因子）；实验在d≤200、三重变化d=100序列及128特征人体活动数据上验证，能检测对均值CUSUM不可见的纯依赖变化。

Conclusion: 该方法在非参数密度设置下有效检测高维分布变化，特别是依赖结构变化，理论上达到最优率（至多对数因子损失），且在实际高维场景中计算可行、检测准确。

Abstract: Detecting distributional changes in high dimension is difficult when neither the pre-change nor post-change density is parametrically specified. We introduce a representation-based approach that retains all degree-at-most-two density information while replacing density estimation by matrix mean estimation. For observations in $[-1,1]^d$, a symmetric feature matrix $H_2(X)\in\R^{(d+1)\times(d+1)}$ is constructed so that $M(f)=\E_f H_2(X)$ is an isometric encoding of the degree-two orthogonal projection of the density. We scan matrix CUSUMs after rank-$r$ truncation, exploiting the low rank of the projected jump rather than sparsity of individual coordinates. The resulting \LRD{} estimator has a tent-shaped population objective and a nonasymptotic operator-norm analysis whose leading stochastic term scales as $\sqrt{rd\log(nd)}$. For multiple changes, we give a seeded narrowest-over-threshold procedure and prove exact recovery by an induction that preserves an isolating interval for every undetected change. A cross-fitted scalar refinement learns the changing low-rank direction on one fold and localizes on the other, attaining $\widetilde O_{\Pp}(κ^{-2})$ error; a matching Le Cam lower bound shows optimality up to logarithms. A geometrically $β$-mixing extension follows from a dependent matrix Bernstein inequality. Experiments with ambient dimension up to $200$, a three-change $d=100$ sequence, and a $128$-feature human-activity benchmark show that the method remains computationally practical and accurately detects pure dependence changes that are invisible to mean CUSUMs.

</details>


### [97] [CForce: Boosting Parallel Decoding for dLLMs via Consistency Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13925)
*Yuji Ren,Chenkai Xu,Zhuocheng Gong,Jianguo Li,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Consistency Forcing (CForce)方法，用于蒸馏扩散大语言模型，使早期阶段的掩码预测与后期阶段对齐，从而改善高并行解码下的速度-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散大语言模型在高并行策略下，早期去噪阶段的掩码预测不可靠，错误会传播到后续阶段，影响生成质量。

Method: CForce通过在预收集的自生成轨迹上训练模型，引入置信度自适应KL散度作为蒸馏目标，结合前向与反向KL的优点。该方法同时适用于掩码到token解码和可编辑解码，并提供理论分析说明一致性目标可近似最小化早期阶段预测误差。

Result: 在非编辑和可编辑LLaDA模型上的实验表明，CForce改善了速度-质量权衡，尤其在高并行解码预算下效果显著。

Conclusion: CForce通过强制早期与后期预测一致来缓解扩散LLM的误差传播问题，是一种有效且通用的蒸馏方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) accelerate language generation by predicting multiple masks in a single forward pass. However, existing dLLMs can suffer from unreliable predictions in early denoising stages under aggressive parallelism strategies, leading to errors that can propagate to later stages. To tackle this issue, we present Consistency Forcing (CForce) for dLLMs, a distillation method to force the mask predictions of early stages to align with those of later stages. CForce trains the model on pre-collected self-rollout trajectories, thereby improving training-inference alignment. We introduce Confidence Adaptive KL Divergence as a distillation objective to conjoin the merits of forward and reverse KL. We further provide a theoretical analysis for the consistency objective to explain why CForce can approximately minimize the prediction error of early stages. Critically, the same formulation applies to both mask-to-token decoding and edit-capable decoding; in the edit-capable case, later token-to-token refinements provide additional supervision for earlier masked-state predictions. Experiments on non-edit and edit-capable LLaDA models show improved speed-quality trade-offs, especially under high-parallelism decoding budgets. Code is available at: https://github.com/inclusionAI/dFactory.

</details>


### [98] [Post-training Quantization for Hybrid Iterative Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13932)
*Jing Gao,Junyi Wu,Wei Wang,Yan Yan,Yao Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HyGenQ，一种针对混合迭代生成模型（IGM）的后训练量化（PTQ）框架，通过层次聚类解耦（HCD）和缩放重新校准（SR）分别解决激活值中的过度异常值（EOs）和放大异常值（AAs）问题，成功实现W8A8量化而不会引发模型崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合迭代生成模型（结合自回归与扩散范式）虽有出色的图像生成保真度，但其迭代推理计算开销巨大，亟需PTQ加速。然而直接应用vanilla PTQ会导致模型崩溃，根源在于两类挑战：激活值中的过度异常值（EOs）造成正常精度与异常值覆盖之间不可调和的权衡；微小量化误差引发的放大异常值（AAs）导致校准与推理失配，迭代触发模型崩溃。

Method: HyGenQ包含两个核心组件：1）层次聚类解耦（HCD），通过多阶段聚类过程识别并解耦异常值通道，既隔离EOs又保持正常值精度；2）缩放重新校准（SR），将AAs缩放到高斯界限之外，避免激进截断导致的模型崩溃。

Result: 大量实验表明，HyGenQ成功将代表性混合IGM量化至8位精度（W8A8），显著优于现有基线方法，并在不同模型家族上验证了其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HyGenQ有效解决了混合迭代生成模型量化中的两大关键挑战（EOs和AAs），为混合IGM提供了一种鲁棒且高效的PTQ方案，同时保持优异的图像生成质量。

Abstract: Iterative Generative Models (IGMs) span autoregressive and diffusion paradigms, and hybrid variants that couple them can achieve remarkable image-generation fidelity. However, their iterative inference incurs substantial computational overhead, making Post-training Quantization (PTQ) appealing for acceleration, while directly applying vanilla PTQ to hybrid IGMs can trigger model collapse. By analyzing these failures, we identify two critical challenges: Excessive Outliers (EOs) in the activations create an irreconcilable trade-off between preserving normal precision and covering EOs, resulting in severe degradation in generation quality; Amplified Anomalies (AAs) arising unpredictably from minor quantization errors, create a mismatch between calibration and inference, thus iteratively triggering model collapse. To address these challenges, we introduce HyGenQ, a PTQ framework for hybrid IGMs. HyGenQ comprises Hierarchical Cluster Decoupling (HCD) and Scaling Recalibration (SR). HCD identifies and decouples outlier channels via a multi-stage clustering process, effectively isolating EOs while maintaining normal value precision, thereby alleviating performance degradation. SR scales AAs beyond Gaussian Bound, thereby avoiding model collapse caused by aggressive truncation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HyGenQ successfully quantizes representative hybrid IGMs to 8-bit precision (W8A8), significantly outperforming existing baselines and validating its robustness across different model families.

</details>


### [99] [Probabilistic indirect models for undrained shear strength: addressing significant data missing and variability with advanced imputation and machine learning techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13934)
*Haibin Xiong,Shaoheng Dai,Peng Lan,Xuzhen He,Chenxi Tong,Sheng Zhang,Daichao Sheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用CLAY/10/7490全球数据库，结合多种缺失值插补方法和多头注意力机制的概率神经网络（MHA-PNN），构建了基于阿太堡界限和CPTU测量的不排水抗剪强度（su）概率间接预测模型。结果表明MN插补增强的MHA-PNN模型在预测精度和不确定性量化方面均显著优于其他模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经验方法预测不排水抗剪强度（su）存在显著不确定性，且数据库常伴有高缺失率和变异性，需要开发更稳健的概率间接预测模型以提高岩土工程设计中的预测精度和可靠性。

Method: 首先使用三种插补方法（MN、MICE、MF）填补CLAY/10/7490数据库的缺失值，通过PXGB模型评估插补效果；然后将多头注意力（MHA）机制集成到人工神经网络（ANN）中，构建MHA-PNN概率模型，并以RMSE、R2、MAPE、wCI和CR等指标评估性能。

Result: MN插补增强的MHA-PNN模型在预测精度（RMSE、R2、MAPE）和不确定性量化（wCI、CR）方面均大幅优于其他对比模型（包括传统MN基础预测模型）。

Conclusion: 集成MN插补与MHA-PNN的混合策略为构建鲁棒的概率间接预测模型提供了可行路径，尤其适用于数据稀疏且不完整的岩土工程场景。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of undrained shear strength (su) is crucial for geotechnical design, but is often hampered by substantial uncertainty in traditional empirical methods. This study uses the CLAY/10/7490 global database to develop probabilistic indirect models to predict su based on Atterberg limits and piezocone cone penetration (CPTU) measurements. Firstly, the dataset has a high missing data rate and variability. We test three imputation methods - multivariate normal (MN), multiple imputation by chained equations (MICE), and miss forest (MF) - to fill the missing values. To validate their effectiveness, a Probabilistic Extreme Gradient Boosting (PXGB) model is developed, and the imputation methods are evaluated by comparing the PXGB's performance when trained on the imputed datasets against that on the original incomplete data. Secondly, the indirect model is built by integrating a multi-head attention (MHA) mechanism into an artificial neural network (ANN) to enhance information extraction from limited data, which leads to the MHA-based probabilistic neural networks (MHA-PNN) model. The models' performance, alongside a conventional MN-based prediction model, was evaluated using root mean square error (RMSE), coefficient of determination (R2), mean absolute percentage error (MAPE), conditional interval width (wCI), and coverage rate (CR). Results demonstrate that the proposed MN-enhanced MHA-PNN model substantially outperforms other models in both prediction accuracy and uncertainty quantification. These findings highlight the potential of this integrated strategy for building robust probabilistic indirect models in geotechnical applications, particularly when confronted with sparse and incomplete datasets.

</details>


### [100] [Polar Code Based Federated Learning: Convergence Analysis and Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13961)
*Han Xiao,Wei Kang,Nan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于极化码的跨层联邦学习（FL）方案，利用极化码在有限块长下的不等差错保护（UEP）特性，优先保护本地模型中更重要的量化比特位，以缓解信道噪声对模型传输的影响，并提供收敛性分析与联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习忽视信道损伤或采用等差错保护（EEP），未考虑单个本地模型内量化比特的重要性差异，导致通信瓶颈和性能损失。

Method: 提出跨层极化码FL方案，利用极化码的UEP特性选择性地保护更重要的量化比特；推导收敛间隙上界，并在所有训练迭代中联合优化量化比特数和极化码块长度。

Result: 实验表明，常量和可变块长度的极化码方案在未编码和LDPC-based EEP基准上均取得显著性能提升，且信道质量越差优势越明显。

Conclusion: 跨层设计能有效增强联邦学习在真实信道条件下的鲁棒性和通信效率。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data; however, it faces significant communication bottlenecks and channel impairments in practice. Conventional network layer treatments either idealize the channel as error free or apply equal error protection (EEP) to transmitted model updates, failing to account for the inherently unequal importance of quantization bits within a single local model. To address this limitation, we propose a cross layer polar code based FL scheme that leverages the unequal error protection (UEP) property of polar codes under finite block lengths. Specifically, the proposed design selectively protects more significant quantization bits, thereby mitigating the detrimental effects of channel noise. We further provide a rigorous convergence analysis of the proposed scheme, deriving an upper bound on the convergence gap, which we then jointly optimize over the number of quantization bits and the polar code block length across all training iterations. Experimental results demonstrate that both constant and variable block length configurations of our polar code based scheme consistently achieve substantial performance gains over uncoded and LDPC-based EEP benchmarks, with the advantage becoming increasingly pronounced as the channel quality deteriorating. These findings confirm the efficacy of our cross-layer design in enhancing FL robustness and efficiency under realistic channel conditions.

</details>


### [101] [QUASAR: Lowering the Loss Floor of Quantization-Aware Training with Loss-Aware Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13966)
*Vincent Counathe,Ben Athiwaratkun,Christopher De Sa,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUASAR是一种量化感知训练(QAT)方法，通过在训练循环中持续进行轻量级的损失感知重建，降低低比特量化模型的损失下限，在Qwen3和Llama-3.1上于2/3/4比特下均达到最优的KL散度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理向低精度发展使得后训练量化(PTQ)变得脆弱，量化感知训练(QAT)成为必要。然而传统QAT在损失计算（使用有损重建的权重）与更新（作用于潜在全精度权重）之间存在不匹配，导致次优训练轨迹和较高损失下限。二阶PTQ方法虽能缓解类似问题，但耗时过长无法在QAT中重复使用。

Method: QUASAR在每步训练中持续执行轻量级损失感知重建：利用梯度平方的指数移动平均作为在线显著性估计，搜索少量裁剪范围，并通过显著性加权最小二乘拟合仿射反量化器。该方法仅修改训练过程，支持标准部署格式（整数量化和NVFP4），无需推理时改动或额外开销。

Result: 在Qwen3和Llama-3.1上，QUASAR在2/3/4比特下均取得最低的保留KL散度，3/4比特下KL至少降低10%，2比特下降低29%。在2比特下，跨8个任务的平均准确率相比强QAT和PTQ基线提升3.5-4.3个百分点。

Conclusion: QUASAR通过将损失感知重建融入QAT训练循环，建立了原理上最优的优化目标，有效降低了低比特量化模型的损失下限，显著提升了量化模型质量，且无需改变推理部署流程。

Abstract: As large language model inference shifts toward lower precision, post-training quantization (PTQ) becomes increasingly brittle, making quantization-aware training (QAT) essential for preserving model quality. However, QAT computes the loss and surrogate gradients using a lossy reconstruction of latent full-precision weights, while applying updates to the latent weights themselves. This mismatch can lead to suboptimal training trajectories and a higher loss floor. Second-order PTQ methods mitigate a similar gap by minimizing loss-aware reconstruction error, but doing it once for a frozen model can take hours; repeating this process throughout QAT as the weights evolve is impractical. We introduce QUASAR, a QAT method that continuously performs lightweight, loss-aware reconstruction in the training loop to lower the loss floor and improve the resulting low-bit model. At each training step, QUASAR uses the exponential moving average of squared gradients as online saliency estimates, searches over a small set of clipping ranges, and fits affine dequantizers via saliency-weighted least squares. Our analysis shows that the loss-aware reconstruction error is the only reconstruction-dependent term in the QAT convergence bound and controls the loss of the final quantized model, establishing QUASAR's objective as a principled optimization target. QUASAR modifies only the training procedure and supports standard deployment formats, including integer quantization and NVFP4, with no inference-time changes or overhead. Across Qwen3 and Llama-3.1, QUASAR achieves the lowest held-out KL divergence among competitive QAT methods at 2, 3, and 4 bits, reducing KL by at least 10% at 3 and 4 bits and by 29% at 2 bits. At 2 bits, it improves average accuracy across eight tasks by 3.5-4.3 percentage points over strong QAT and PTQ baselines.

</details>


### [102] [Identifiability and Order-Dimension Limits of In-Context Learning on Partial Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14004)
*Faizanuddin Ansari,Debanjan Dutta,Swagatam Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了偏序集上上下文学习的理论框架，区分了逻辑可辨识性、提示教学成本、结构复杂性和形式化坐标解码器类的精确容量，提出了开放世界提示的完成三分法定理，并刻画了教学数与阻塞集命中数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文学习形式化通常假设从函数示例中进行推理，但偏序集结合了传递性、反对称性和不可比性，有限的提示可能无法确定查询的比较结果，需要专门的理论来处理这一复杂性。

Method: 采用版本空间语义学，明确区分背景知识与开放/封闭世界假设；对开放世界提示（含正负比较示例）证明精确的完成三分法；通过覆盖数加阻塞集命中数刻画教学数；形式化提示依赖的s-坐标解码器并利用经典坐标序等价性建立表示边界。

Result: 证明了对有限开放世界提示的完成三分法：查询要么被迫为真、要么因循环或违反负示例被迫为假、要么真正模糊；教学数的最大值在n元素偏序集上为n(n-1)，由反链唯一达到；阻塞项恰为开放世界语义相对完全Hasse语义的额外代价；维度至多s是必要且充分条件，宽度至多s是充分条件。

Conclusion: 本文为偏序上的上下文学习提供了完整的理论刻画，将逻辑可辨识性、教学成本、结构复杂性与解码器表达能力精确分离，揭示了开放世界与封闭世界语义之间的本质差异。

Abstract: In-context learning is commonly formalized as inference from examples of a function. Partial orders instead combine transitivity, antisymmetry, and incomparability, so a finite prompt may not determine a queried comparison. We develop a theory of in-context learning on partial orders that separates logical identifiability, prompt teaching cost, structural complexity, and the exact capacity of a formal coordinate-decoder class. A version-space semantics makes background knowledge and open- versus closed-world assumptions explicit. For finite open-world prompts with positive and negative comparisons, we prove an exact completion trichotomy: after taking the reflexive transitive closure of the positive demonstrations, a query is forced true, forced false because every true completion creates a cycle or violates a negative demonstration, or remains genuinely ambiguous. For a known $n$-element universe, we characterize the open-world teaching number as the number of covers plus a blocker-set hitting number, prove that its maximum over all $n$-element posets is $n(n-1)$ and is uniquely attained by the antichain, and identify the blocker term as the exact cost of open-world rather than complete-Hasse semantics. We formalize prompt-dependent $s$-coordinate decoders and use the classical coordinate-order equivalence to obtain an exact representation boundary: dimension at most $s$ is necessary and sufficient, while width at most $s$ is a convenient sufficient condition.

</details>


### [103] [When Does More Correct Data Hurt? Insertion-Stability and the Limits of Dimension-Based Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14020)
*Joseph Sankoorikal Johny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究插入稳定性（insertion-stable）学习器在单调对抗者（monotone adversary）下的鲁棒性。单调对抗者可以在正确标记的训练数据后追加任意数量的正确标记样本，这可能导致学习器性能下降。作者证明插入稳定学习器（即喂入更多正确标记样本只会缩小错误区域的学习器）对这类对抗者免疫，但同时指出经典维度（VC维、Littlestone维度）无法预测这种免疫性，且某些类（如Mehrotra的硬类）上任何有限大小的单调置换不变压缩方案都无法达到干净速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明，在单调对抗者场景下，VC维为d>=2的类无法获得优于Theta((d/n)log(en/d))的期望错误率，比干净PAC速率多一个对数因子。但该速率为所有类的最坏情况，无法区分哪些类真正付出代价。本文旨在厘清：哪些类会遭受惩罚，哪些不会，以及这取决于学习器的什么性质。

Method: 作者引入"插入稳定学习器"的概念——若向学习器输入更多正确标记样本只会缩小其出错区域，则该学习器对单调对抗者免疫。基于此定义，作者分析插入稳定性与经典维度（VC维、Littlestone维度）的关系，并通过构造在VCdim=Ldim=2上却表现截然不同的类来证明经典维度无法预测免疫性。此外，作者在Mehrotra硬类上证明了任何有限大小的单调置换不变压缩方案都无法达到干净速率。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）插入稳定学习器对单调对抗者完全免疫，高概率保证可原样继承；（2）Closure算子是插入稳定的，因此所有交闭类保持干净速率E[Err] <= (21d+34)/n；（3）存在VCdim=Ldim=2的两个类，一个达到Theta(1/n)另一个仅为Theta(log(en)/n)，说明经典维度无法预测免疫性；（4）区间类具有无界Littlestone维度但免疫；（5）在Mehrotra硬类上，任何有限大小的单调置换不变压缩方案均无法达到干净速率。

Conclusion: 问题的关键不在于类是否困难，也不在于学习器是否优秀，而在于两者是否匹配。若给定一个在干净数据上最优的插入稳定学习器，正确添加的数据是免费的；若没有这样的学习器，代价归属于类本身，更换学习器也无法避免。这重新定义了对抗性鲁棒性的归因框架。

Abstract: Adding data known to be correct ought to be safe. Not always. Larsen, Pabbaraju and Shetty model the failure with a monotone adversary, which reads an i.i.d. training sample and may append as many further examples as it likes, provided the target hypothesis labels them all. Mehrotra has since settled the cost, showing that for classes of VC dimension d >= 2 no learner can guarantee expected error better than Theta((d/n)log(en/d)), a logarithmic factor above the clean PAC rate.
  Because that rate is a worst case over all classes, it says nothing about which classes actually suffer the penalty, and the answer turns on the learner. We call a learner insertion-stable if feeding it more correctly labeled examples can only shrink the region where it errs. Such learners are immune to the adversary, since on any given sample the risk after insertions never exceeds the risk on the clean part alone, however much is added and however cleverly it is chosen. High- probability guarantees carry over unchanged, and because Closure is insertion-stable every intersection-closed class keeps its clean rate of E[Err] <= (21d+34)/n.
  Immunity is not something the classical dimensions can predict. Two classes can agree on VCdim = Ldim = 2 and still split, one at Theta(1/n) and the other at Theta(log(en)/n), while intervals have unbounded Littlestone dimension and are immune anyway. On Mehrotra's hard class we prove more than the failure of a single algorithm, showing that no monotone permutation-invariant compression scheme of any finite size attains the clean rate.
  The question is therefore not whether a class is hard, nor whether a learner is good, but whether the two suit each other. Given an insertion-stable learner that is optimal on clean data, correct additions are free, and without one the cost belongs to the class, so changing the learner will not avoid it.

</details>


### [104] [Adversarial Learning of Classifier-Free Guidance Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14038)
*Ashwini Pokle,Alexandre Galashov,Arnaud Doucet,Mauricio Delbracio,Valentin De Bortoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种学习文本到图像扩散模型中分类器自由引导（CFG）动态调度的方法，将引导调度建模为扩散时间、条件和当前噪声样本的函数，通过密度比估计训练判别器，并由轻量级生成器网络预测最优的状态依赖引导尺度，在基准测试上优于启发式CFG调度和既有动态引导学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CFG采用静态全局引导尺度，对所有时间步、样本和条件统一应用，这通常是次优的，不同状态可能需要不同程度的引导。虽然时间变化调度已知能提升质量，但手工设计困难且依赖具体应用场景。

Method: 将引导调度学习建模为密度比估计问题：训练一个判别器估计真分布与引导边缘分布之间的时间依赖对数密度比；同时训练一个轻量级生成器网络预测最优的、状态依赖的引导尺度，该尺度是扩散时间、条件和当前噪声样本的函数。

Result: 实验表明该方法在文本到图像生成基准上优于启发式CFG调度以及先前的动态引导学习方法。

Conclusion: 学习状态依赖的引导调度比静态或手工设计的引导调度更优，能够在不同扩散状态下自适应调整引导强度，从而更好地对齐生成图像与文本提示，同时减少伪影。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image diffusion models rely on classifier-free guidance (CFG) to achieve high image fidelity and text alignment. However, CFG typically applies a static, global scale across all timesteps, samples, and conditions -- a choice that is generally suboptimal and can introduce artifacts, as different states may benefit from different levels of guidance. While time-varying schedules are known to improve quality, designing them by hand is non-trivial and application-dependent. In this paper, we learn the guidance schedule as a function of diffusion time, conditioning and the current noisy sample, in order to better align sampled images with the text prompt. We frame this as a density ratio estimation problem: a discriminator is trained to estimate the time-dependent log-density ratio between the true and guided marginal distributions, while a lightweight generator network predicts the optimal, state-dependent guidance scale. Empirically, our approach outperforms both heuristic CFG schedules and prior methods for learning dynamic guidance on text-to-image generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [105] [Model-agnostic Retrieval-Augmented Extended Forecasting for time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14054)
*Juan Pablo Villa Serna,Rohan Asthana,Vasileios Belagiannis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RAEF（检索增强扩展预测），一种基于RAF改进的模型无关方法，用于时间序列预测中的域自适应。通过直接在输入空间检索而非嵌入空间，以及采用拼接方式而非平均方式聚合检索结果，RAEF在准确性和推理开销上都优于RAF，并能在避免微调计算成本的同时达到与其相当或更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练基础模型在零样本时间序列预测中表现出色，但在历史数据很少的领域特定应用中需要微调或RAG来适配。微调虽然有效但计算成本高昂，因此需要更高效的替代方案。

Method: RAEF基于RAF构建，包含两个关键改进：(1)在输入空间而非嵌入空间进行直接检索，减少推理开销；(2)采用基于拼接的聚合而非平均方式，保留时间结构信息。RAEF是模型无关的，可适用于不同的预测基础模型。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实证评估表明，RAEF在准确性和推理开销两方面均优于RAF。与零样本和微调基础模型的全面比较显示，RAEF能达到与微调相当或更优的性能，同时避免了微调的计算负担。

Conclusion: RAEF是一种实用且可扩展的时间序列预测域自适应方法，在保持高性能的同时显著降低了计算成本，为缺乏历史数据的领域特定时间序列预测提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasting with pretrained foundation models has demonstrated strong zero-shot capabilities. However, achieving optimal performance on time series with short or negligible historical data in domain-specific applications typically requires adaptation via either fine-tuning or RAG. While fine-tuning is effective, it incurs substantial computational costs. This work explores RAG within univariate time series (Retrieval Augmented Generation) as a more efficient alternative, in particular RAF (Retrieval Augmented Forecasting), and introduces RAEF (Retrieval-Augmented Extended Forecasting), a model-agnostic method built upon RAF. RAEF incorporates key refinements to the retrieval and aggregation mechanisms: (1) direct retrieval in input-space rather than embedding-space, reducing inference overhead, and (2) concatenation-based aggregation that preserves temporal structure instead of averaging. Empirical evaluation across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrates that RAEF outperforms RAF in both accuracy and inference overhead. Furthermore, comprehensive comparisons with zero-shot and fine-tuned foundation models show that RAEF achieves competitive or superior performance to fine-tuning while avoiding its computational burden, establishing it as a practical and scalable approach for domain adaptation in time series forecasting.

</details>


### [106] [When Denoising Hurts: Rethinking the Terminal Step of Diffusion Time Series Forecasters -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14067)
*Dat Nguyen-Cong,Luong Tran,Tung Kieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑扩散模型在时间序列预测中"采样迭代处处有益"的传统观点，发现时间序列的一般结构在较高噪声水平即可恢复，而低噪声下的继续细化反而引入统计漂移并降低预测质量。基于此提出无标签的全局停止准则来自动检测最优终止点，并设计伯努利时间步采样器将训练集中在高噪声区域，在八个真实数据集上验证了优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型被广泛应用于时间序列预测以建模不确定性，但其迭代采样过程通常被视为均匀有益的细化步骤，缺乏对其各阶段质量演变的深入分析。作者希望挑战这一假设，揭示低噪声细化阶段可能带来的负面影响。

Method: 1) 分析反向扩散过程中预测质量的演变规律，发现一般时间结构在高噪声水平即可恢复，低噪声细化会引入统计漂移；2) 提出无标签的全局停止准则，自动检测逆扩散的最优终止点以加速推理并提升精度；3) 提出伯努利时间步采样器，将训练重心聚焦于高噪声区域同时保持对完整扩散过程的覆盖。

Result: 在八个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法相比现有方法具有更优的预测性能，同时通过提前终止实现了推理加速。

Conclusion: 扩散模型在时间序列预测中的低噪声细化阶段并非总是有益的，合理地进行提前停止不仅能加快推理速度，还能提升预测精度。这一发现为设计更高效的扩散时间序列预测模型提供了新的理论视角和实践指导。

Abstract: Diffusion models offer a natural way to model uncertainty in time series forecasting, yet their iterative sampling process is often treated as a uniformly beneficial refinement procedure. Our study challenges this view by examining how forecast quality evolves throughout reverse diffusion. We find that general temporal structure is often recovered at relatively high noise levels, whereas continued low-noise refinement can introduce statistical drift and degrade the final forecast. Our analysis further suggests that this behavior explains why prior methods often favor relatively narrow diffusion architecture and schedule design. Building on this observation, we propose a label-free global stopping criterion that detects the optimal termination point, eventually speeding up inference and improving predictive accuracy.
